 351: | --- | --- | --- |
 352: | Current wallet settings | `ShadowProductionReplayService` injects `WalletSettingsRepository`; `walletSettings()` loads row `1`. | Replay reads only frozen, extracted fixture settings. |
 353: | Live exchange constraints | Replay calls `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate` for sell/harvest checks; it reaches Binance exchange-info data. | Fixture-backed historical exchange constraints; no live HTTP in Replay. |
 354: | Shared diagnostics/evidence | `RegressionTestService` reads and executes `DELETE FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NOT NULL`; `StopLossEvidenceStore` inserts/reads that table and can read `wallet_trade`. | Enforce the exact run-owned `fix122_evaluation` exception/delete policy in Section 4.2a; all other shared access is forbidden and must be proven by allow-list and reachability tests. |
 355: | Production trade enrichment/charting | `RegressionTestService.allTradesChart` and proven-trade detail paths query/enrich `wallet_trade`. | Historical extraction before a Replay run or a Replay-local replacement. |
 356: | Profit-lock extension | Replay applies the current admin enable flag; Production forces enabled during extension rebase. | Frozen historical profile and exact Production input/precedence. |
 357: | Opportunity lifecycle | Replay invokes `completePositionOpportunity` for price-driven exits as well as signal-driven exits. | Mirror the two Production decision orders and lifecycle transitions. |
 358: | Signal identity/consumption | Scope is thread-local; regenerated signals may have null ids. | Replay-owned stable signal key and consumption state, while preserving saved source ids. |
 359: | Parity measurement | Existing tests are limited and no demonstrated full decision-by-decision RPR-0.9.11 parity validator is invoked. | Registered comparator, divergence report, critical-safety gate, and fixture suite. |
 360: 
 361: Current discovered Replay-oriented tests include
 362: `ShadowProductionReplayServiceFix120ParityTest`, `ReplayDatasetTest`, and
 363: `ExecutionReplayScopeModeTest`. They do not demonstrate completion of
 364: T156-01 through T156-16. In particular, the existing FIX-120 test mocks both
 365: `WalletSettingsRepository` and `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy`, confirming
 366: that those prohibited runtime dependencies are still part of the current
 367: Replay design.
 368: 
 369: ### 11.5 Mandatory gates before implementation
 370: 
 371: 1. Complete P01 with exact source, configuration, migration, and clean-worktree hashes.
 372: 2. Review the exact RPR-0.9.11 revision and resolve all material findings.
 373: 3. Complete E03-E10 as read-only measured evidence, including historical settings, price-event coverage, signal availability, policy fingerprints, and resource limits.
 374: 4. Update the preparation registry only where that completed evidence requires it, while keeping RPR-0.9.11 as the canonical contract.
 375: 5. Obtain explicit user authorization for the exact reviewed and frozen revision.
 376: 
 377: The recorded P01 status reports a large dirty working tree. It cannot serve
 378: as a reproducible baseline until the hashes and a clean baseline are
 379: recorded.
 380: 
 381: ### 11.6 Implementation boundary after authorization
 382: 
 383: FIX-156 may change Replay-owned code, fixtures, isolated Replay tables,
 384: tests, and documentation only. It must not change Production trading logic,
 385: Production wallet behaviour, Production database records, live Binance
 386: behaviour, or deployment configuration.
 387: 
 388: The future implementation must include the registered structured logs with
 389: UTC and KSA values, distinguish `ANALYSIS_PARITY` from `LIFECYCLE_PARITY`,
 390: treat a business mismatch as parity-not-earned rather than `FAILED_REPLAY`,
 391: and reject a parity claim for a critical-safety mismatch.
 392: 
 393: ### 11.7 Consolidated conclusion
 394: 
 395: This document is now the single consolidated FIX-156 preparation and final
 396: pre-implementation review artifact. It does not approve implementation.
 397: Current code materially differs from the FIX-156 target by design. The next
 398: safe action is completion of the evidence-and-freeze gates, followed by an
 399: explicit implementation authorization for this exact RPR-0.9.11 revision or a
 400: newer fully reviewed revision.

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java:1 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
   6: import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;
   7: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
   8: import javax.sql.DataSource;
   9: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  10: import java.time.Instant;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: 
  14: /** FIX-122: bounded, non-locking committed ledger reads; no managed-position row locks.
  15:  * STOP_LOSS is the terminal reason written by executeSignalLinkedExit. The distinct
  16:  * POSITION_STOP_LOSS and partial-harvest reasons are deliberately not included.
  17:  */
  18: @Service
  19: public class StopLossEvidenceStore {
  20:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  21:     private final TransactionTemplate read;
  22:     private final TransactionTemplate write;
  23: 
  24:     public StopLossEvidenceStore(DataSource dataSource, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  25:         jdbc = new JdbcTemplate(dataSource);
  26:         jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
  27:         read = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  28:         read.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  29:         read.setIsolationLevel(TransactionDefinition.ISOLATION_READ_COMMITTED);
  30:         read.setReadOnly(true);
  31:         read.setTimeout(2);
  32:         write = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  33:         write.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  34:         write.setTimeout(2);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary latest(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  38:         return read.execute(status -> jdbc.query("""
  39:                 SELECT id, executed_at FROM wallet_trade
  40:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED' AND side='SELL'
  41:                   AND execution_reason='STOP_LOSS' AND executed_at<=?
  42:                 ORDER BY executed_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1
  43:                 """, (rs, row) -> new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(rs.getLong(1),
  44:                 rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()), symbol, Timestamp.from(asOf))
  45:                 .stream().findFirst().orElse(null));
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     public void append(Long runId, String symbol, Long signalId, Instant at, String stage, String payload) {
  49:         // Separate audit transaction survives a caller rollback; it records an evaluation,
  50:         // never asserts that a BUY/SELL actually executed. No FK to wallet rows is acquired.
  51:         write.executeWithoutResult(status -> jdbc.update("""
  52:                 INSERT INTO fix122_evaluation(test_run_id,symbol,signal_id,evaluated_at,stage,payload)
  53:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)
  54:                 """, runId, symbol, signalId, Timestamp.from(at), stage, payload));
  55:     }
  56: 
  57:     public List<Map<String,Object>> replayDetails(long runId) {
  58:         return jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id=? ORDER BY id", runId);
  59:     }
  60: 
  61:     public List<Map<String,Object>> productionDetails(String symbol, Instant from, Instant to) {
  62:         return jdbc.queryForList("""
  63:                 SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NULL AND symbol=?
  64:                 AND evaluated_at>=? AND evaluated_at<=? ORDER BY id
  65:                 """, symbol, Timestamp.from(from), Timestamp.from(to));
  66:     }
  67: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/Fix122ProvenSnapshot.java:1 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   4: import java.sql.Timestamp;
   5: import java.time.Instant;
   6: 
   7: /** FIX-122: preserve the reviewed evidence with the Proven record. Replay purge/reset
   8:  * can reuse run IDs, so a saved Proven trade must not resolve diagnostics by run ID later. */
   9: public final class Fix122ProvenSnapshot {
  10:     private Fix122ProvenSnapshot() {}
  11:     public static void capture(JdbcTemplate jdbc,long provenId,Long runId,String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
  12:         jdbc.update("""
  13:                 UPDATE proven_analyzed_trade SET fix122_diagnostics_json = JSON_OBJECT(
  14:                     'scope', 'PROVEN',
  15:                     'enabled', (SELECT enabled FROM fix122_replay_revision WHERE test_run_id=?),
  16:                     'rows', COALESCE((SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT(
  17:                         'evaluated_at', d.evaluated_at, 'signal_id', d.signal_id,
  18:                         'stage', d.stage, 'payload', d.payload))
  19:                         FROM fix122_evaluation d WHERE d.symbol=? AND d.evaluated_at BETWEEN ? AND ?
  20:                         AND ((? IS NULL AND d.test_run_id IS NULL) OR d.test_run_id=?)), JSON_ARRAY()))
  21:                 WHERE id=?
  22:                 """,runId,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to),runId,runId,provenId);
  23:     }
  24: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/EntryConsumptionPolicy.java:1 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.execution.domain.EntryConsumptionState;
   4: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletTradeRepository;
   5: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * FIX-112A: consumption belongs to the specific bullish signal, not to the
  10:  * symbol's general position state. A pre-existing position can coexist with a
  11:  * blocked progressive-add signal, so OPEN-position state is not proof that the
  12:  * immediately previous bullish signal actually executed.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  16: public class EntryConsumptionPolicy {
  17:     private final WalletTradeRepository walletTradeRepository;
  18:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  19: 
  20:     public EntryConsumptionState resolve(Long previousSignalId) {
  21:         if (previousSignalId == null) return EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  22:         if (replayScope.active()) {
  23:             return replayScope.entryConsumed(previousSignalId)
  24:                     ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED
  25:                     : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  26:         }
  27:         // Production authority is the immutable executed BUY row keyed to the
  28:         // exact triggering signal. This works for both first entries and adds.
  29:         boolean executed = walletTradeRepository
  30:                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
  31:                         previousSignalId, "BUY", "EXECUTED")
  32:                 .isPresent();
  33:         return executed ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  34:     }
  35: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:1 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.Comparator;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.Optional;
  12: import java.util.HashSet;
  13: import java.util.Set;
  14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;

  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }

 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }

 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }

 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }

   1: # Replay / Production Business-Logic-Parity Redesign
   2: 
   3: **Revision:** RPR-0.4-DRAFT  
   4: **Status:** DRAFT ƒ?" NOT APPROVED ƒ?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
   5: **Created:** 2026-10-10  
   6: **Scope owner:** Codex implementation / Claude independent review
   7: 
   8: ## 1. Business objective
   9: 
  10: Make manual Replay reproduce the current paper-trading Production business flow
  11: as closely as durable historical evidence permits, while remaining completely
  12: isolated from Production trading state and wallet data.
  13: 
  14: The exact intended flow is:
  15: 
  16: ```text
  17: historical candle + historical context + canonical accepted price events
  18:   -> shared Production indicator/signal/decision policies
  19:   -> replay-local virtual wallet/account state
  20:   -> shared sizing/allocation/entry revalidation policies
  21:   -> replay-local position lifecycle
  22:   -> shared TP/SL/profit-lock/continuation/exit policies
  23:   -> replay output + explicit parity certificate
  24: ```
  25: 
  26: Replay must never write `wallet_*`, `paper_position`, `trade_signal`,
  27: `signal_processing_work`, or any other Production trading table.
  28: 
  29: The result is not a claim that historical Java scheduling, MySQL lock timing,
  30: network delay, external Binance availability, or the historical Production
  31: wallet balance can be reproduced. It is a verified **business-logic parity**
  32: claim only when all required market/decision evidence exists. Otherwise Replay
  33: remains available as a labelled simulation and may not claim parity.
  34: 
  35: ## 2. Confirmed root cause and evidence
  36: 
  37: The 2026-10-10 source audit established the following:
  38: 
  39: 1. Signal, entry, normal-exit, continuation, profit-lock, and near-TP policy
  40:    use several shared Production services.
  41: 2. `ShadowProductionReplayService` separately owns replay wallet cash,
  42:    positions, daily budget, persistence, price-loop lifecycle, and result
  43:    labels. It starts at `INITIAL_CAPITAL=10000`, runs one symbol/position, and
  44:    does not reproduce Production wallet/global controls.
  45: 3. A run is labelled `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` when at least one accepted price
  46:    event exists, rather than proving coverage for every interval while a
  47:    position was open.
  48: 4. Historical derivatives information is not persisted;
  49:    `DerivativesPositioningService.evaluateHistorical()` returns UNAVAILABLE.
  50:    Historical order-book evaluation can likewise be unavailable when snapshots
  51:    were not retained.
  52: 5. Replay-only FIX-11K and FIX-11Q observers, and the pre-FIX-122 UI revision,
  53:    have no current Production equivalent and must not contaminate parity runs.
  54: 6. Current Replay reads `WalletSettingsRepository` directly and its near-TP
  55:    path can call `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy`, which can request a current
  56:    live Binance minimum notional. Both violate replay account/external-input
  57:    isolation.
  58: 
  59: ## 3. Boundaries
  60: 
  61: ### In scope
  62: 
  63: - Replay input/evidence qualification and run-level parity status.
  64: - Replay-local virtual wallet state, wallet controls, positions, executions,
  65:   daily statistics, and global limit reproduction.
  66: - Canonical price-event coverage qualification and event ordering.
  67: - Historical context-evidence qualification for derivatives and order book.
  68: - Removal of approved retired Replay-only business/experiment behaviour.
  69: - Parity certificate, diagnostics, migrations, tests, documentation.
  70: 
  71: ### Out of scope
  72: 
  73: - Any Production strategy change, sizing change, exit change, or wallet change.
  74: - Live-exchange orders, fills, fees, slippage, or order-book execution modelling.
  75: - Replaying database locks, thread scheduling, JVM pauses, WebSocket transport,
  76:   external REST failures, or transaction retries as historical facts.
  77: - FIX-153, FIX-154, and FIX-155 trading-policy changes.
  78: - Deleting Production evidence or existing historical Replay/archive rows.
  79: 
  80: ## 4. Contractual modes
 288: | RPR-T-06 | Multi-symbol global wallet/max-position test proves no isolated-symbol bypass. |
 289: | RPR-T-07 | Cancellation/error tests prove Production tables remain unchanged and certificates cannot falsely pass. |
 290: | RPR-T-08 | MySQL migration and transaction tests use a disposable database. |
 291: | RPR-T-09 | Dataset and database replay inputs produce equivalent output for the same qualified evidence, or one path is removed. |
 292: | RPR-T-10 | Removal tests prove baseline Replay cannot invoke FIX-11K, FIX-11Q, or pre-FIX-122 paths. |
 293: | RPR-T-11 | Real historical parity suite compares selected known Production windows and produces stored certificates. |
 294: 
 295: ## 12. Risks, rollback, and acceptance
 296: 
 297: - Risk: historical data required for verified parity may not exist. Mitigation:
 298:   refuse parity and use clearly labelled simulation; do not invent evidence.
 299: - Risk: snapshot schema increases storage. Mitigation: bounded retention and
 300:   archive strategy, never deletion of active/investigation evidence.
 301: - Risk: retiring observers breaks historical UI/archive reads. Mitigation:
 302:   migration and archive/export compatibility tests before removal.
 303: - Rollback: disable new business-logic-parity mode and retain existing archived
 304:   output; Production requires no rollback because it is never mutated.
 305: 
 306: Acceptance requires: all registry IDs reconciled; no unregistered code change;
 307: all test evidence green; disposable MySQL validation; complete source review by
 308: Claude; Codex verification of the exact reviewed revision; and explicit user
 309: authorization after both agents approve this same document.
 310:

 541:                         0, current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
 542:                 persistNearTpState(current);

 547:             }
 548:             BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(price, MC);
 549:             BigDecimal soldCost = current.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 550:             BigDecimal partialPnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 551:             BigDecimal partialPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : partialPnl

 560:     }

 562:     private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
 563:         BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
 564:         if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
 565: 
 566:         // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
 567:         // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
 568:         // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
 569:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
 570:         if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 571:             // Exact production TP-continuation call, including immutable entry structure.
 572:             // FIX-011 regression relies on Replay and live using this same shared policy.
 573:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 574:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 575:                     p.entryTrend(), p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(),
 576:                     p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());
 577:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 578:                 BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
 579:                 BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 580:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
 581:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
 582:                 return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
 583:             }
 584:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
 585:                     runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 586:                     p.entryTime(), p.entryPrice(), p.quantity(), p.cost(), p.stopLoss(), p.takeProfit(), p.highest(),
 587:                     p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.entryScore(), p.entryConfidence(), p.entryTrend(),
 588:                     p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(), oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 589:                     continuation.explanation());
 590:             persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), p, continuation.explanation());
 591:             return new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation());
 592:         }
 593:         // FIX-052 fallback ordering also mirrors Production: STOP_LOSS is absolute and
 594:         // is evaluated before profit-lock. Exact V64 runs normally take this path from
 595:         // persisted live-price events rather than from signal-carried prices.
 596:         if (authoritativePrice && p.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(p.stopLoss()) <= 0)
 597:             return new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the stored stop loss.");
 598: 
 599:         ShadowPosition updated = authoritativePrice ? profitLockState(p, price) : p;
 600:         if (authoritativePrice && (updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE
 601:                 || updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 602:                 || p.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()
 603:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice())
 604:                 || p.profitLockState() != updated.profitLockState())) {
 605:             BigDecimal fix118Progress = updated.takeProfit() == null || updated.takeProfit().compareTo(updated.entryPrice()) <= 0
 606:                     ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 607:                     : updated.highest().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC)
 608:                             .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 609:                             .divide(updated.takeProfit().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 610:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_PATH] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, " +
 611:                             "entry={}, target={}, highest={}, previousActive={}, active={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousLock={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, triggered={}",
 612:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 613:                     updated.entryPrice(), updated.takeProfit(), updated.highest(), p.profitLockActive(), updated.profitLockActive(),
 614:                     p.profitLockState(), updated.profitLockState(), p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice(), fix118Progress,
 615:                     updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 616:                             && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0);
 617:         }
 618:         BigDecimal minimumProfitableExit = p.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
 619:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 620:                 && price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 621:             String floorReason = "TP-extension rebase safety floor was breached at " + minimumProfitableExit + ". "
 622:                     + profitLockConfigText();
 623:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, floor={}",
 624:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price, minimumProfitableExit);
 625:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 626:             return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 627:         }
 628:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 629:                 && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
 630:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, lock={}, target={}",
 631:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 632:                     updated.profitLockPrice(), updated.takeProfit());
 633:             if (price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 634:                 String floorReason = "Profit-lock hard floor was breached; protected profit can no longer be preserved. "
 635:                         + profitLockConfigText();
 636:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 637:                 return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 638:             }
 639:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(
 640:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 641:             if (!lockDecision.exit()) {
 642:                 String holdReason = lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 643:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, holdReason);
 644:                 return ExitDecision.hold();
 645:             }
 646:             String lockReason = "Price retraced to the protected profit level after a profitable advance. "
 647:                     + lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 648:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, lockReason);
 649:             return new ExitDecision(true, lockDecision.code(), lockReason);
 650:         }
 651:         // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the
 652:         // 1m signal TradeExecutionValidationService. Replay executes both shared production
 653:         // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.
 654:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(
 655:                 oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 656:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 657:             return new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation());
 658:         }
 659:         if ("1m".equals(s.getInterval()) && bearish(s.getDecision())) {
 660:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(s);
 661:             if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
 662:                 return new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
 663:             }
 664:         }
 665:         return ExitDecision.hold();
 666:     }

 668:     private FallbackNearTpEvaluation evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(long runId, ShadowPosition current,
 669:                                                                     TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal oneMinute,
 670:                                                                     TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 671:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 672:                 current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
 673:                 current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
 674:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 675:                 state, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt(),
 676:                 oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 677:         ShadowPosition updated = current.withNearTp(evaluation.state());
 678:         if (!updated.sameNearTp(current)) persistNearTpState(updated);
 679:         if (evaluation.transition()) {
 680:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
 681:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), updated.nearTpState(),
 682:                     signal.getLatestPrice(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), updated.nearTpBearishStreak(), evaluation.explanation());
 683:         }
 684:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 685: 
 686:         BigDecimal soldQty = updated.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
 687:         BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
 688:                 binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, signal.getLatestPrice());
 689:         if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
 690:             updated = updated.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 691:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, updated.nearTpBestPrice(),
 692:                     0, updated.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
 693:             persistNearTpState(updated);
 694:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
 695:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
 696:                     minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
 697:             return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 698:         }
 699:         BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
 700:         BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 701:         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 702:         BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 703:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 704:         updated = updated.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, pnl);
 705:         persistNearTpPartialHarvestAtSignal(runId, signal, updated, soldQty, proceeds, pnl, pnlPct, evaluation.explanation());
 706:         log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, price={}, partialPnl={}",
 707:                 runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, updated.quantity(), signal.getLatestPrice(), pnl);
 708:         return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, proceeds, pnl);
 709:     }

 209:     /**
 210:      * FIX-120 correction/refinement to FIX-118. Called in the same transaction that persists the new TP.
 211:      * A TP change invalidates the prior executable lock as a calculation baseline. If current
 212:      * best progress remains at/above activation, stay ACTIVE but derive a fresh lock from the
 213:      * new geometry using raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as the historical
 214:      * best seed. If progress falls below activation, preserve the historical lock for audit but

 217:     public ExtensionTransition onTakeProfitExtended(WalletManagedPosition position, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant changedAt) {
 218:         if (position == null || newTarget == null || position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt() == null) {
 219:             return ExtensionTransition.none();
 220:         }
 221:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
 222:         BigDecimal oldTarget = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 223:         BigDecimal oldLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();

 225:                 ? position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt()
 226:                 : position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
 227:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) {
 228:             extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 229:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(extensionHighest);
 230:         }
 231:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 232:         BigDecimal activation = configuredProfile(settings()).activationPercent();
 233:         ProfitLockState nextState = previousState;
 234:         // TP changed now, so persist progress against the new geometry immediately even though
 235:         // the extension tick returns before the next normal Profit Lock evaluation.
 236:         position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
 237: 
 238:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 239:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 240:                 nextState = ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 241:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 242:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt);
 243:                 // Legacy compatibility flag deliberately stays true; ProfitLockState is the sole
 244:                 // authority for whether the persisted historical lock may execute.
 245:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 246:             } else {
 247:                 // FIX-120: the extension still leaves the winner beyond activation, but the old
 248:                 // lock belongs to the superseded TP geometry. Establish a fresh geometry baseline.
 249:                 // Deliberately pass raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as previous
 250:                 // highest: ProfitLockPolicy then sees the real boundary tick plus the authoritative
 251:                 // best price, while previousActive/previousLock are intentionally discarded.
 252:                 ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings());
 253:                 BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;

 259:                 position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(rebased.progressPercent());
 260:                 position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(rebased.lockPrice());
 261:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 262:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 263:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(nextState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 264:                         ? (changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt)
 265:                         : null);
 266:                 progress = rebased.progressPercent();
 267:             }
 268:         } else if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 269:             // Repeated TP extensions keep the rebase active and refresh progress against the

 106:     private Optional<PaperPosition> processSignalBody(TradeSignal signal) {
 107:         if (signal == null) {

 109:         }
 110: 
 111:         String symbol = normalizeSymbol(signal.getSymbol());
 112: 
 113:         // FIX-127: position management persists advice and can request POSITION_STOP_LOSS.
 114:         // Keep its existing decision order, but acquire the initial managed lock before entering here.
 115:         if (positionManagementService != null) {
 116:             try {
 117:                 positionManagementService.analyze(signal);
 118:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 119:                 log.warn("Position advisory failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage());
 120:             }
 121:         }

 123:                 .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN);

 130:             // Context from that signal is still processed below, but TP/SL/profit-lock

 132:             // is independently driven by LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice(...).

 138: 
 139:             // FIX-118 Production trace for the independent signal-price Profit Lock authority.

 141:             // or temporarily non-executable while TP extension is rebasing.

  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
 105:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
 106:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 107:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
 108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 109:         };

 153:     /**
 154:      * FIX-11T: read the symbol's current Binance minimum executable notional directly
 155:      * from exchangeInfo.  Near-TP uses this only as a minimum-order guard; it does not
 156:      * introduce a separate quantity-rounding model. NOTIONAL is preferred when present,
 157:      * with MIN_NOTIONAL retained for symbols that still expose the older filter.
 158:      */
 159:     public BigDecimal getMinimumExecutableNotional(String symbol) {
 160:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 161:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 162:         }
 163: 
 164:         Map<String, Object> response = request(() -> restClient.get()
 165:             .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder
 166:                 .path("/api/v3/exchangeInfo")
 167:                 .queryParam("symbol", symbol.toUpperCase())
 168:                 .build())
 169:             .retrieve()
 170:             .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<>() {
 171:             }));
 172: 
 173:         if (response == null) {
 174:             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty Binance exchangeInfo response for " + symbol);
 175:         }
 176:         Object symbolsValue = response.get("symbols");
 177:         if (!(symbolsValue instanceof List<?> symbols) || symbols.isEmpty() || !(symbols.get(0) instanceof Map<?, ?> symbolInfo)) {
 178:             throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not contain symbol rules for " + symbol);
 179:         }
 180:         Object filtersValue = symbolInfo.get("filters");
 181:         if (!(filtersValue instanceof List<?> filters)) {
 182:             throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not contain filters for " + symbol);
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         BigDecimal minNotional = null;
 186:         for (Object filterValue : filters) {
 187:             if (!(filterValue instanceof Map<?, ?> filter)) continue;
 188:             String filterType = String.valueOf(filter.get("filterType"));
 189:             if (!"NOTIONAL".equals(filterType) && !"MIN_NOTIONAL".equals(filterType)) continue;
 190:             Object raw = filter.get("minNotional");
 191:             if (raw == null) continue;
 192:             BigDecimal candidate = new BigDecimal(String.valueOf(raw));
 193:             if (candidate.signum() <= 0) continue;
 194:             if ("NOTIONAL".equals(filterType)) return candidate;
 195:             minNotional = candidate;
 196:         }
 197:         if (minNotional != null) return minNotional;
 198:         throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not expose a minimum notional for " + symbol);
 199:     }

 209:     /**
 210:      * FIX-120 correction/refinement to FIX-118. Called in the same transaction that persists the new TP.
 211:      * A TP change invalidates the prior executable lock as a calculation baseline. If current
 212:      * best progress remains at/above activation, stay ACTIVE but derive a fresh lock from the
 213:      * new geometry using raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as the historical
 214:      * best seed. If progress falls below activation, preserve the historical lock for audit but
 215:      * make it non-executable in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE until the current geometry requalifies.
 216:      */
 217:     public ExtensionTransition onTakeProfitExtended(WalletManagedPosition position, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant changedAt) {
 218:         if (position == null || newTarget == null || position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt() == null) {
 219:             return ExtensionTransition.none();
 220:         }
 221:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
 222:         BigDecimal oldTarget = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 223:         BigDecimal oldLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
 224:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null
 225:                 ? position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt()
 226:                 : position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
 227:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) {
 228:             extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 229:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(extensionHighest);
 230:         }
 231:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 232:         BigDecimal activation = configuredProfile(settings()).activationPercent();
 233:         ProfitLockState nextState = previousState;
 234:         // TP changed now, so persist progress against the new geometry immediately even though
 235:         // the extension tick returns before the next normal Profit Lock evaluation.
 236:         position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
 237: 
 238:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 239:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 240:                 nextState = ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 241:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 242:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt);
 243:                 // Legacy compatibility flag deliberately stays true; ProfitLockState is the sole
 244:                 // authority for whether the persisted historical lock may execute.
 245:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 246:             } else {
 247:                 // FIX-120: the extension still leaves the winner beyond activation, but the old
 248:                 // lock belongs to the superseded TP geometry. Establish a fresh geometry baseline.
 249:                 // Deliberately pass raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as previous
 250:                 // highest: ProfitLockPolicy then sees the real boundary tick plus the authoritative
 251:                 // best price, while previousActive/previousLock are intentionally discarded.
 252:                 ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings());
 253:                 BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
 254:                 ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 255:                         position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
 256:                         false, null, true, profile.activationPercent(), profile.initialLockPercent(), profile.trailStepPercent());
 257:                 nextState = rebased.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 258:                 position.setHighestPriceUsdt(rebased.highestPrice());
 259:                 position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(rebased.progressPercent());
 260:                 position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(rebased.lockPrice());
 261:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 262:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 263:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(nextState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 264:                         ? (changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt)
 265:                         : null);
 266:                 progress = rebased.progressPercent();
 267:             }
 268:         } else if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 269:             // Repeated TP extensions keep the rebase active and refresh progress against the

 711:     private ShadowPosition updateProfitLock(ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s) {
 712:         if (s.getLatestPrice() == null || !priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime())) return p;
 713:         ShadowPosition n = profitLockState(p, s.getLatestPrice());
 714:         if (!equalsNullable(p.highest(), n.highest()) || p.profitLockActive() != n.profitLockActive()
 715:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockPrice())
 716:                 || p.profitLockState() != n.profitLockState()
 717:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt(), n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt())) {
 718:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET highest_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_active=?, profit_lock_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_state=?, profit_lock_rebase_started_at=? WHERE id=?",

 720:                     n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt()), p.positionId());
 721:         }

 725:     private ShadowPosition profitLockState(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 726:         WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 727:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(settings.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 728:         BigDecimal highest = p.highest() == null ? price : p.highest().max(price);
 729:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), highest);
 730: 
 731:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 732:                 && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 733:             // Replay parity with Production FIX-118: preserve the historical lock/legacy flag,
 734:             // but keep it non-executable until the new TP geometry earns activation again.
 735:             return p.withLock(highest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 736:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 737:         }
 738: 
 739:         // FIX-118 parity: when REBASE earns activation, rebuild the lock from the current
 740:         // TP geometry instead of retaining the superseded pre-extension lock.
 741:         boolean freshGeometryActivation = p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 742:         ProfitLockPolicy.State state = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 743:                 p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), price, highest,
 744:                 freshGeometryActivation ? false : (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || p.profitLockActive()),
 745:                 freshGeometryActivation ? null : p.profitLockPrice(),
 746:                 settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 747:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 748:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 749:         ProfitLockState nextState = state.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 750:         return p.withLock(state.highestPrice(), state.active(), state.lockPrice(), nextState, null);
 751:     }

  98:                 baselinePnl, baselinePnlPct, "ACTIVE", baselineExplanation);
  99: 
 100:         log.info("FIX11Q_GRACE_ACTIVATED runId={} positionId={} symbol={} observedAt={} candle={} price={} tp={} current={} five={} one={} trend={}/{} momentum={}/{} action=HOLD_TARGET_UNCHANGED",
 101:                 runId, positionId, symbol, observedAt, candleIdentity, price, takeProfit,
 102:                 name(current1m.getDecision()), name(fiveMinute.getDecision()), name(oneHour.getDecision()),
 103:                 current1m.getTrendScore(), trendFloor(entryTrend), current1m.getMomentumScore(), momentumFloor(entryMomentum));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public synchronized void onSignal(long runId, TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal latest1m, TradeSignal latest5m, TradeSignal latest1h) {
 107:         Variant v = activeByRun.get(runId);
 108:         if (v == null || signal == null) return;
 109:         if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next1mDecision() == null && !sameGraceCandle(v, signal)) {
 110:             v = v.withNext1m(name(signal.getDecision()));
 111:         }
 112:         if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next5mDecision() == null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null && signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) {
 113:             v = v.withNext5m(name(signal.getDecision()));
 114:         }
 115:         if ("1h".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next1hDecision() == null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null && signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) {
 116:             v = v.withNext1h(name(signal.getDecision()));
 117:         }
 118:         activeByRun.put(runId, v.withContext(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h));
 119:     }
 120: 
 121:     public synchronized void onPrice(long runId, String symbol, MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent event,
 122:                                      TradeSignal latest1m, TradeSignal latest5m, TradeSignal latest1h) {
 123:         Variant v = activeByRun.get(runId);
 124:         if (v == null || event == null || event.price() == null || !v.symbol().equals(symbol)) return;
 125:         if (!event.observedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) return;
 126: 
 127:         BigDecimal price = event.price();
 128:         v = v.withContext(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h).withPath(price);
 129:         boolean sameGraceCandle = sameGraceCandle(v, latest1m);
 130: 
 131:         // Hard protection remains active even during grace; FIX-11Q suppresses only the baseline
 132:         // TAKE_PROFIT exit caused by transient continuation failure.
 133:         if (v.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(v.stopLoss()) <= 0) {
 134:             close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "STOP_LOSS", "Stop loss reached during/after grace."); return;
 135:         }
 136: 
 137:         v = updateProfitLock(v, price);
 138:         if (v.profitLockActive() && v.profitLockPrice() != null && price.compareTo(v.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
 139:             BigDecimal hardFloor = v.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
 140:             if (price.compareTo(hardFloor) < 0) {
 141:                 close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", "Profit-lock hard floor breached in FIX-11Q counterfactual."); return;
 142:             }
 143:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lock = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 144:             if (lock.exit()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, lock.code(), lock.explanation()); return; }
 145:         }
 146: 
 147:         if (v.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(v.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 148:             if (sameGraceCandle) {
 149:                 activeByRun.put(runId, v); // hold entire candle; target intentionally unchanged
 150:                 return;
 151:             }
 152:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 153:                     latest1m, latest5m, latest1h, v.entryTrend(), v.entryStructure(), v.entryMomentum(), v.entryVolume(),
 154:                     v.entryConfidence(), v.entryScore());
 155:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 156:                 BigDecimal distance = v.takeProfit().subtract(v.entryPrice());
 157:                 BigDecimal newTarget = v.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 158:                 activeByRun.put(runId, v.withTakeProfit(newTarget));
 159:                 log.info("FIX11Q_VARIANT_TP_EXTENDED runId={} positionId={} oldTp={} newTp={} reason={}", runId, v.positionId(), v.takeProfit(), newTarget, continuation.explanation());
 160:                 return;
 161:             }
 162:             close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation()); return;
 163:         }
 164: 
 165:         if (!sameGraceCandle) {
 166:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normal = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 167:             if (normal.exit()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, normal.code(), normal.explanation()); return; }
 168:             if (latest1m != null && "1m".equals(latest1m.getInterval()) && bearish(latest1m.getDecision())) {
 169:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validated = executionValidationService.validateSell(latest1m);
 170:                 if (validated.allowed()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, validated.code(), validated.explanation()); return; }
 171:             }
 172:         }
 173:         activeByRun.put(runId, v);
 174:     }
 175: 
 176:     public synchronized void finishRun(long runId, String symbol, Instant endTime) {
 177:         Variant v = activeByRun.remove(runId);
 178:         if (v != null) {
 179:             BigDecimal pnl = v.lastPrice().multiply(v.quantity(), MC).subtract(v.cost(), MC);
 180:             updateRow(runId, v, endTime, v.lastPrice(), "OPEN_AT_WINDOW_END", pnl, percentage(v.entryPrice(), v.lastPrice()));
 181:         }
 182:         Integer count = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM one_candle_continuation_grace_test WHERE test_run_id=?", Integer.class, runId);
 183:         log.info("FIX11Q_GRACE_SUMMARY runId={} symbol={} candidates={} mode=REPLAY_COUNTERFACTUAL", runId, symbol, count == null ? 0 : count);
 184:         consumedByRun.remove(runId);
 185:     }
 186: 
 187:     private boolean eligible(TradeSignal one, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal hour, int entryMomentum) {
 188:         return one != null && five != null && hour != null
 189:                 && one.getDecision() == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 190:                 && bullish(hour.getDecision()) && !bearish(five.getDecision())
 191:                 && one.getMomentumScore() >= momentumFloor(entryMomentum);
 192:     }
 193: 
 194:     private Variant updateProfitLock(Variant v, BigDecimal price) {
 195:         WalletSettings s = walletSettingsRepository.findById(1L).orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 196:                 .id(1L).dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true).profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 197:                 .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40)).profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10)).build());
 198:         ProfitLockPolicy.State st = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(v.entryPrice(), v.takeProfit(), price, v.highest(),
 199:                 v.profitLockActive(), v.profitLockPrice(), s.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(),
 200:                 nvl(s.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70)),
 201:                 nvl(s.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 202:                 nvl(s.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 203:         return v.withLock(st.highestPrice(), st.active(), st.lockPrice());
 204:     }

 206:     private void close(long runId, Variant v, Instant at, BigDecimal price, String reason, String explanation) {
 207:         BigDecimal pnl = price.multiply(v.quantity(), MC).subtract(v.cost(), MC);
 208:         updateRow(runId, v, at, price, reason, pnl, percentage(v.entryPrice(), price));
 209:         activeByRun.remove(runId);
 210:         log.info("FIX11Q_VARIANT_CLOSED runId={} positionId={} symbol={} at={} price={} reason={} baselinePnl={} variantPnl={} delta={}",
 211:                 runId, v.positionId(), v.symbol(), at, price, reason, v.baselinePnl(), pnl, pnl.subtract(v.baselinePnl(), MC));
 212:     }

 219:                   variant_exit_reason=?,variant_pnl_usdt=?,variant_pnl_percent=?,pnl_delta_usdt=?,

 505:     /**
 506:      * FIX-11S: one read-only chart payload for an entire Replay run. Replay executions are
 507:      * taken from the isolated wallet_execution_test rows; Production executions are only
 508:      * queried for the same symbol and requested Replay window. An empty Production result
 509:      * is a normal case and is deliberately returned as [] rather than null.
 510:      */
 511:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 512:     public Map<String, Object> allTradesChart(long runId, String interval) {
 513:         List<Map<String, Object>> runs = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 514:                 SELECT id, symbol, start_time, end_time
 515:                 FROM analysis_test_run
 516:                 WHERE id = ?
 517:                 """, runId);
 518:         if (runs.isEmpty()) {
 519:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Replay run not found.");
 520:         }
 521: 
 522:         Map<String, Object> run = runs.get(0);
 523:         String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol")).trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 524:         Timestamp start = (Timestamp) run.get("start_time");
 525:         Timestamp end = (Timestamp) run.get("end_time");
 526:         String normalizedInterval = switch (interval == null ? "5m" : interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
 527:             case "1m", "5m", "1h", "4h" -> interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
 528:             default -> "5m";
 529:         };
 530: 
 531:         List<Map<String, Object>> candles = candleReader(jdbcTemplate).queryForList("""
 532:                 SELECT open_time, open_price, high_price, low_price, close_price, volume
 533:                 FROM candle
 534:                 WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?
 535:                 ORDER BY open_time ASC
 536:                 """, symbol, normalizedInterval, start, end);
 537: 
 538:         List<Map<String, Object>> replayExecutions = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 539:                 SELECT id, side, execution_time, execution_price, quantity, notional_usdt,
 540:                        position_percent, signal_interval, signal_decision, execution_source,
 541:                        execution_code, execution_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent
 542:                 FROM wallet_execution_test
 543:                 WHERE test_run_id=? AND symbol=?
 544:                   AND execution_time BETWEEN ? AND ?
 545:                   AND UPPER(side) IN ('BUY','SELL')
 546:                 ORDER BY execution_time ASC, id ASC
 547:                 """, runId, symbol, start, end);
 548: 
 549:         List<Map<String, Object>> productionExecutions = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 550:                 SELECT id, signal_id, side, executed_at AS execution_time, price_usdt AS execution_price,
 551:                        quantity, gross_amount_usdt, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent,
 552:                        execution_type, execution_reason, execution_message
 553:                 FROM wallet_trade
 554:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED'
 555:                   AND executed_at BETWEEN ? AND ?
 556:                   AND UPPER(side) IN ('BUY','SELL')
 557:                 ORDER BY executed_at ASC, id ASC
 558:                 """, symbol, start, end);
 559: 
 560:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 561:         result.put("runId", runId);
 562:         result.put("symbol", symbol);
 563:         result.put("interval", normalizedInterval);
 564:         result.put("from", start);
 565:         result.put("to", end);
 566:         result.put("candles", candles);
 567:         result.put("replayExecutions", replayExecutions);
 568:         result.put("productionExecutions", productionExecutions);
 569:         return result;
 570:     }

 604:     public Map<String, Object> markProvenSuccess(long runId, long tradeId) {
 605:         List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 606:                 SELECT id, test_run_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price, exit_reason,

 608:                 FROM wallet_position_test

 610:                 """, runId, tradeId);

 612:         Map<String, Object> t = rows.get(0);
 613:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 614:                 INSERT INTO proven_analyzed_trade
 615:                     (source_test_run_id, source_trade_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price,
 616:                      exit_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent, position_percent)
 617:                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
 618:                 ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE symbol=VALUES(symbol), entry_time=VALUES(entry_time), entry_price=VALUES(entry_price),
 619:                     exit_time=VALUES(exit_time), exit_price=VALUES(exit_price), exit_reason=VALUES(exit_reason),
 620:                     realized_pnl_usdt=VALUES(realized_pnl_usdt), realized_pnl_percent=VALUES(realized_pnl_percent),
 621:                     position_percent=VALUES(position_percent), marked_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 622:                 """,
 623:                 runId, tradeId, t.get("symbol"), t.get("entry_time"), t.get("entry_price"), t.get("exit_time"),
 624:                 t.get("exit_price"), t.get("exit_reason"), t.get("realized_pnl_usdt"), t.get("realized_pnl_percent"),
 625:                 t.get("position_percent"));
 626:         // FIX-122: snapshot before a later Replay purge can remove/reuse this run identity.
 627:         Long provenId=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT id FROM proven_analyzed_trade WHERE source_test_run_id=? AND source_trade_id=?",Long.class,runId,tradeId);
 628:         com.crypto.execution.service.Fix122ProvenSnapshot.capture(jdbcTemplate,provenId,runId,(String)t.get("symbol"),
 629:                 ((Timestamp)t.get("entry_time")).toInstant().minusSeconds(1800),((Timestamp)t.get("exit_time")).toInstant());
 630:         return Map.of("saved", true, "runId", runId, "tradeId", tradeId);
 631:     }

 655:     /**
 656:      * Archive only one reviewed execution leg. This is intentionally separate from
 657:      * regression-run archiving: a reviewer can preserve the BUY or SELL point on its
 658:      * own without freezing/copying the complete test run. Clear Data still keeps the
 659:      * existing full-run safety archive so replay diagnostics remain recoverable.
 660:      */
 661:     @Transactional
 662:     public Map<String, Object> archiveProvenTradeLeg(long provenTradeId, String requestedSide) {
 663:         String side = requestedSide == null ? "" : requestedSide.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 664:         if (!side.equals("BUY") && !side.equals("SELL")) {
 665:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Archive side must be BUY or SELL.");
 666:         }
 667:         List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 668:                 SELECT id, source_test_run_id, source_trade_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price,
 669:                        exit_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent
 670:                 FROM proven_analyzed_trade
 671:                 WHERE id=?
 672:                 """, provenTradeId);
 673:         if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Proven trade not found.");
 674:         Map<String, Object> trade = rows.get(0);
 675:         Object time = side.equals("BUY") ? trade.get("entry_time") : trade.get("exit_time");
 676:         Object price = side.equals("BUY") ? trade.get("entry_price") : trade.get("exit_price");
 677:         if (time == null || price == null) {
 678:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, side + " leg is not available for this proven trade.");
 679:         }
 680:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 681:                 INSERT INTO proven_trade_leg_archive
 682:                     (proven_trade_id, source_test_run_id, source_trade_id, symbol, side, execution_time, execution_price,
 683:                      exit_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent)
 684:                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
 685:                 ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE execution_time=VALUES(execution_time), execution_price=VALUES(execution_price),
 686:                     exit_reason=VALUES(exit_reason), realized_pnl_usdt=VALUES(realized_pnl_usdt),
 687:                     realized_pnl_percent=VALUES(realized_pnl_percent), archived_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 688:                 """, provenTradeId, trade.get("source_test_run_id"), trade.get("source_trade_id"), trade.get("symbol"),
 689:                 side, time, price, side.equals("SELL") ? trade.get("exit_reason") : null,
 690:                 side.equals("SELL") ? trade.get("realized_pnl_usdt") : null,
 691:                 side.equals("SELL") ? trade.get("realized_pnl_percent") : null);
 692:         return Map.of("archived", true, "provenTradeId", provenTradeId, "side", side);
 693:     }

 556:     private void recordReplayStage(ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage stage, long startedNs) {
 557:         if (profiler != null) {
 558:             profiler.record(stage, System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 559:         }
 560:     }

 562:     /**
 563:      * FIX-11M categories intentionally follow the existing buildSignal call order.
 564:      * They are measurement labels only and are never consulted by trading logic.
 565:      */
 566:     private enum ReplayAnalysisStage {
 567:         SENTIMENT("sentiment"),
 568:         FUNDAMENTALS("fundamentals"),
 569:         PREVIOUS_SNAPSHOT("previousSnapshot"),
 570:         TREND_STRUCTURE("trendStructure"),
 571:         BASE_SCORING("baseScoring"),
 572:         ATR_RISK("atrRisk"),
 573:         REGIME("regime"),

 575:         STRATEGY("strategy"),

 578:         DERIVATIVES("derivatives"),
 579:         ORDER_BOOK("orderBook"),
 580:         ENTRY_AUTHORITY("entryAuthority"),

 582:         FINAL_DECISION("finalDecision"),
 583:         SIGNAL_ASSEMBLY("signalAssembly");
 584: 
 585:         private final String label;
 586: 
 587:         ReplayAnalysisStage(String label) {
 588:             this.label = label;
 589:         }
 590:     }
 591: 
 592:     private static final class ReplayAnalysisProfiler {
 593:         private final long runId;
 594:         private final EnumMap<ReplayAnalysisStage, ReplayStageTiming> stages = new EnumMap<>(ReplayAnalysisStage.class);
 595:         private long callNs;
 596:         private long calls;
 597:         private long maxCallNs;
 598: 
 599:         private ReplayAnalysisProfiler(long runId) {
 600:             this.runId = runId;
 601:             for (ReplayAnalysisStage stage : ReplayAnalysisStage.values()) {
 602:                 stages.put(stage, new ReplayStageTiming());
 603:             }
 604:         }
 605: 
 606:         private void recordCall(long elapsedNs) {
 607:             callNs += elapsedNs;
 608:             calls++;
 609:             maxCallNs = Math.max(maxCallNs, elapsedNs);
 610:         }
 611: 
 612:         private void record(ReplayAnalysisStage stage, long elapsedNs) {
 613:             ReplayStageTiming timing = stages.get(stage);
 614:             timing.totalNs += elapsedNs;
 615:             timing.calls++;
 616:             timing.maxNs = Math.max(timing.maxNs, elapsedNs);
 617:         }
 618: 
 619:         private void logSummary(org.slf4j.Logger logger, long requestedRunId) {
 620:             long measuredNs = stages.values().stream().mapToLong(t -> t.totalNs).sum();
 621:             long otherNs = Math.max(0L, callNs - measuredNs);
 622:             StringBuilder message = new StringBuilder(
 623:                     "FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE run={} profileRun={} totalMs={} calls={} avgMs={} maxMs={}");
 624:             java.util.List<Object> args = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 625:             args.add(requestedRunId);
 626:             args.add(runId);
 627:             args.add(millis(callNs));
 628:             args.add(calls);
 629:             args.add(averageMillis(callNs, calls));
 630:             args.add(millis(maxCallNs));
 631: 
 632:             for (ReplayAnalysisStage stage : ReplayAnalysisStage.values()) {
 633:                 ReplayStageTiming timing = stages.get(stage);
 634:                 message.append(' ').append(stage.label).append("Ms={}")
 635:                         .append(' ').append(stage.label).append("Calls={}")
 636:                         .append(' ').append(stage.label).append("AvgMs={}")
 637:                         .append(' ').append(stage.label).append("MaxMs={}");
 638:                 args.add(millis(timing.totalNs));
 639:                 args.add(timing.calls);
 640:                 args.add(averageMillis(timing.totalNs, timing.calls));
 641:                 args.add(millis(timing.maxNs));
 642:             }
 643:             message.append(" otherMs={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false");
 644:             args.add(millis(otherNs));
 645:             logger.info(message.toString(), args.toArray());
 646:         }
 647: 
 648:         private static String millis(long ns) {
 649:             return String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", ns / 1_000_000.0d);
 650:         }
 651: 
 652:         private static String averageMillis(long ns, long calls) {
 653:             return calls == 0L ? "0.000"
 654:                     : String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", (ns / 1_000_000.0d) / calls);
 655:         }
 656:     }
 657: 
 658:     private static final class ReplayStageTiming {
 659:         private long totalNs;
 660:         private long calls;
 661:         private long maxNs;
 662:     }
