  30:     public StrategyProfile select(MarketRegimeAssessment assessment, MarketContextSnapshot context) {
  31:         if (!properties.isEnabled()) {
  32:             return profile(TradingStrategy.TREND_FOLLOWING, properties.getTrendFollowing(), true,
  33:                     "Dynamic strategy selection is disabled; using the default profile");
  34:         }
  35:         if (context != null && !context.dataValid()) {
  36:             return profile(TradingStrategy.NO_TRADE, properties.getDefensive(), false,
  37:                     "Required market data is invalid or incomplete; new entries are disabled");
  38:         }
  39:         if (context != null && context.liquidityRisky()) {
  40:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  41:                     "Current order-book liquidity risk selected the defensive profile");
  42:         }
  43:         if (context != null && (context.derivativesStatus() == com.crypto.domain.DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED
  44:                 || context.derivativesStatus() == com.crypto.domain.DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED)) {
  45:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  46:                     "Extreme futures positioning selected the defensive profile");
  47:         }
  48:         if (context != null && context.sentimentEnabled()
  49:                 && context.sentimentCoverage().compareTo(java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50)) < 0) {
  50:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  51:                     "Less than half of enabled sentiment providers are contributing; defensive profile selected");
  52:         }
  53:         if (context != null && context.higherTimeframeStatus() == com.crypto.domain.ConfluenceStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT) {
  54:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  55:                     "Strong higher-timeframe conflict selected the defensive profile");
  56:         }
  57:         if (context != null
  58:                 && context.btcContextStatus() == com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
  59:                 && context.btcInfluenceFactor() != null
  60:                 && context.btcInfluenceFactor().compareTo(java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(0.70)) >= 0) {
  61:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  62:                     "Strong BTC conflict for a highly correlated asset selected the defensive profile");
  63:         }
  64:         if (assessment.confidence() < properties.getMinimumRegimeConfidence()) {
  65:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  66:                     "Regime confidence is below the configured minimum; defensive profile selected");
  67:         }
  68: 
  69:         return switch (assessment.regime()) {
  70:             case STRONG_UPTREND, WEAK_UPTREND, STRONG_DOWNTREND, WEAK_DOWNTREND ->
  71:                     profile(TradingStrategy.TREND_FOLLOWING, properties.getTrendFollowing(), true,
  72:                             "Directional market structure selected the trend-following profile");
  73:             case RANGE -> profile(TradingStrategy.RANGE_MEAN_REVERSION, properties.getRangeMeanReversion(), true,
  74:                     "Range conditions selected the mean-reversion profile");
  75:             case BREAKOUT -> profile(TradingStrategy.BREAKOUT, properties.getBreakout(), true,
  76:                     "Price expansion, volume and structural confirmation selected the breakout profile");
  77:             case BREAKOUT_CANDIDATE -> profile(TradingStrategy.BREAKOUT, properties.getBreakout(), true,
  78:                     "Price expansion and volume detected an early breakout candidate; structural confirmation is still pending");
  79:             case HIGH_VOLATILITY, LOW_LIQUIDITY -> profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  80:                     "Risk conditions selected the defensive profile");
  81:             case UNKNOWN -> profile(TradingStrategy.NO_TRADE, properties.getDefensive(), false,
  82:                     "Market regime is unknown; new entries are disabled");
  83:         };
  84:     }

 591: 
 592:     private static final class ReplayAnalysisProfiler {
 593:         private final long runId;
 594:         private final EnumMap<ReplayAnalysisStage, ReplayStageTiming> stages = new EnumMap<>(ReplayAnalysisStage.class);
 595:         private long callNs;
 596:         private long calls;
 597:         private long maxCallNs;
 598: 
 599:         private ReplayAnalysisProfiler(long runId) {
 600:             this.runId = runId;
 601:             for (ReplayAnalysisStage stage : ReplayAnalysisStage.values()) {
 602:                 stages.put(stage, new ReplayStageTiming());
 603:             }
 604:         }
 605: 
 606:         private void recordCall(long elapsedNs) {
 607:             callNs += elapsedNs;
 608:             calls++;
 609:             maxCallNs = Math.max(maxCallNs, elapsedNs);
 610:         }
 611: 
 612:         private void record(ReplayAnalysisStage stage, long elapsedNs) {
 613:             ReplayStageTiming timing = stages.get(stage);
 614:             timing.totalNs += elapsedNs;
 615:             timing.calls++;
 616:             timing.maxNs = Math.max(timing.maxNs, elapsedNs);
 617:         }
 618: 
 619:         private void logSummary(org.slf4j.Logger logger, long requestedRunId) {
 620:             long measuredNs = stages.values().stream().mapToLong(t -> t.totalNs).sum();
 621:             long otherNs = Math.max(0L, callNs - measuredNs);
 622:             StringBuilder message = new StringBuilder(
 623:                     "FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE run={} profileRun={} totalMs={} calls={} avgMs={} maxMs={}");
 624:             java.util.List<Object> args = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 625:             args.add(requestedRunId);
 626:             args.add(runId);
 627:             args.add(millis(callNs));
 628:             args.add(calls);
 629:             args.add(averageMillis(callNs, calls));
 630:             args.add(millis(maxCallNs));
 631: 
 632:             for (ReplayAnalysisStage stage : ReplayAnalysisStage.values()) {
 633:                 ReplayStageTiming timing = stages.get(stage);
 634:                 message.append(' ').append(stage.label).append("Ms={}")
 635:                         .append(' ').append(stage.label).append("Calls={}")
 636:                         .append(' ').append(stage.label).append("AvgMs={}")
 637:                         .append(' ').append(stage.label).append("MaxMs={}");
 638:                 args.add(millis(timing.totalNs));
 639:                 args.add(timing.calls);
 640:                 args.add(averageMillis(timing.totalNs, timing.calls));
 641:                 args.add(millis(timing.maxNs));
 642:             }
 643:             message.append(" otherMs={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false");
 644:             args.add(millis(otherNs));
 645:             logger.info(message.toString(), args.toArray());
 646:         }
 647: 
 648:         private static String millis(long ns) {
 649:             return String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", ns / 1_000_000.0d);
 650:         }
 651: 
 652:         private static String averageMillis(long ns, long calls) {
 653:             return calls == 0L ? "0.000"
 654:                     : String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", (ns / 1_000_000.0d) / calls);
 655:         }
 656:     }
 657: 
 658:     private static final class ReplayStageTiming {
 659:         private long totalNs;
 660:         private long calls;
 661:         private long maxNs;
 662:     }

  30:     public StrategyProfile select(MarketRegimeAssessment assessment, MarketContextSnapshot context) {
  31:         if (!properties.isEnabled()) {
  32:             return profile(TradingStrategy.TREND_FOLLOWING, properties.getTrendFollowing(), true,
  33:                     "Dynamic strategy selection is disabled; using the default profile");
  34:         }
  35:         if (context != null && !context.dataValid()) {
  36:             return profile(TradingStrategy.NO_TRADE, properties.getDefensive(), false,
  37:                     "Required market data is invalid or incomplete; new entries are disabled");
  38:         }
  39:         if (context != null && context.liquidityRisky()) {
  40:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  41:                     "Current order-book liquidity risk selected the defensive profile");
  42:         }
  43:         if (context != null && (context.derivativesStatus() == com.crypto.domain.DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED
  44:                 || context.derivativesStatus() == com.crypto.domain.DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED)) {
  45:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  46:                     "Extreme futures positioning selected the defensive profile");
  47:         }
  48:         if (context != null && context.sentimentEnabled()
  49:                 && context.sentimentCoverage().compareTo(java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50)) < 0) {
  50:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  51:                     "Less than half of enabled sentiment providers are contributing; defensive profile selected");
  52:         }
  53:         if (context != null && context.higherTimeframeStatus() == com.crypto.domain.ConfluenceStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT) {
  54:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  55:                     "Strong higher-timeframe conflict selected the defensive profile");
  56:         }
  57:         if (context != null
  58:                 && context.btcContextStatus() == com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
  59:                 && context.btcInfluenceFactor() != null
  60:                 && context.btcInfluenceFactor().compareTo(java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(0.70)) >= 0) {
  61:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  62:                     "Strong BTC conflict for a highly correlated asset selected the defensive profile");
  63:         }
  64:         if (assessment.confidence() < properties.getMinimumRegimeConfidence()) {
  65:             return profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  66:                     "Regime confidence is below the configured minimum; defensive profile selected");
  67:         }
  68: 
  69:         return switch (assessment.regime()) {
  70:             case STRONG_UPTREND, WEAK_UPTREND, STRONG_DOWNTREND, WEAK_DOWNTREND ->
  71:                     profile(TradingStrategy.TREND_FOLLOWING, properties.getTrendFollowing(), true,
  72:                             "Directional market structure selected the trend-following profile");
  73:             case RANGE -> profile(TradingStrategy.RANGE_MEAN_REVERSION, properties.getRangeMeanReversion(), true,
  74:                     "Range conditions selected the mean-reversion profile");
  75:             case BREAKOUT -> profile(TradingStrategy.BREAKOUT, properties.getBreakout(), true,
  76:                     "Price expansion, volume and structural confirmation selected the breakout profile");
  77:             case BREAKOUT_CANDIDATE -> profile(TradingStrategy.BREAKOUT, properties.getBreakout(), true,
  78:                     "Price expansion and volume detected an early breakout candidate; structural confirmation is still pending");
  79:             case HIGH_VOLATILITY, LOW_LIQUIDITY -> profile(TradingStrategy.DEFENSIVE, properties.getDefensive(), true,
  80:                     "Risk conditions selected the defensive profile");
  81:             case UNKNOWN -> profile(TradingStrategy.NO_TRADE, properties.getDefensive(), false,
  82:                     "Market regime is unknown; new entries are disabled");
  83:         };
  84:     }

 117:     @Transactional
 118:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecoveredForProcessing(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant at) {
 119:         TradeSignal signal = analyzeRecovered(indicator, at);
 120:         // This legacy LIVE-mode recovery route is execution-capable; it must not
 121:         // retain the historical-only marker used by non-executing catch-up rows.
 122:         signal.setSignalMode("RECOVERY_EXECUTABLE");
 123:         signal = signalRepository.save(signal);
 124:         processingStore.register(signal, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.RECOVERY);
 125:         return signal;
 126:     }

 128:     /**
 129:      * Manual entry point used by controllers or recovery jobs.
 130:      * It reads the latest already-persisted technical indicator.
 131:      */
 132:     @Transactional
 133:     public TradeSignal analyze(String symbol, String interval) {
 134:         TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
 135:                 .getLatest(symbol, interval)
 136:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException(
 137:                         "No technical indicator found for " + symbol + " " + interval
 138:                 ));
 139: 
 140:         return analyze(indicator);
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /**
 144:      * Automatic entry point. It receives the TechnicalIndicator row that was
 145:      * just saved by TechnicalIndicatorService and never recalculates candles.
 146:      */
 147:     @Transactional
 148:     public TradeSignal analyze(TechnicalIndicator indicator) {
 149:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 150:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, Instant.now(), false, false);
 151:         // FIX-153: physical createdAt and persisted mode distinguish live latency from as-of history.
 152:         signal.setSignalMode("LIVE");
 153:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     /**
 157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
 158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
 159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
 165:      */
 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }
 177: 
 178:     /**
 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
 691:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, updated.nearTpBestPrice(),
 692:                     0, updated.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
 693:             persistNearTpState(updated);
 694:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
 695:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
 696:                     minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
 697:             return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 698:         }
 699:         BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
 700:         BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 701:         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 702:         BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 703:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 704:         updated = updated.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, pnl);
 705:         persistNearTpPartialHarvestAtSignal(runId, signal, updated, soldQty, proceeds, pnl, pnlPct, evaluation.explanation());
 706:         log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, price={}, partialPnl={}",
 707:                 runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, updated.quantity(), signal.getLatestPrice(), pnl);
 708:         return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, proceeds, pnl);
 709:     }

 711:     private ShadowPosition updateProfitLock(ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s) {
 712:         if (s.getLatestPrice() == null || !priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime())) return p;
 713:         ShadowPosition n = profitLockState(p, s.getLatestPrice());
 714:         if (!equalsNullable(p.highest(), n.highest()) || p.profitLockActive() != n.profitLockActive()
 715:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockPrice())
 716:                 || p.profitLockState() != n.profitLockState()
 717:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt(), n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt())) {
 718:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET highest_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_active=?, profit_lock_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_state=?, profit_lock_rebase_started_at=? WHERE id=?",
 719:                     n.highest(), n.profitLockActive(), n.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockState().name(),
 720:                     n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt()), p.positionId());
 721:         }
 722:         return n;
 723:     }
 724: 
 725:     private ShadowPosition profitLockState(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 726:         WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 727:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(settings.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 728:         BigDecimal highest = p.highest() == null ? price : p.highest().max(price);
 729:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), highest);
 730: 
 731:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 732:                 && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 733:             // Replay parity with Production FIX-118: preserve the historical lock/legacy flag,
 734:             // but keep it non-executable until the new TP geometry earns activation again.
 735:             return p.withLock(highest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 736:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 737:         }
 738: 
 739:         // FIX-118 parity: when REBASE earns activation, rebuild the lock from the current
 740:         // TP geometry instead of retaining the superseded pre-extension lock.
 741:         boolean freshGeometryActivation = p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 742:         ProfitLockPolicy.State state = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 743:                 p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), price, highest,
 744:                 freshGeometryActivation ? false : (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || p.profitLockActive()),
 745:                 freshGeometryActivation ? null : p.profitLockPrice(),
 746:                 settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 747:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 748:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 749:         ProfitLockState nextState = state.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 750:         return p.withLock(state.highestPrice(), state.active(), state.lockPrice(), nextState, null);
 751:     }
 752: 
 753:     private ShadowPosition rebaseForExtendedTarget(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant at) {
 754:         if (p == null || newTarget == null) return p;
 755:         BigDecimal oldTarget = p.takeProfit();
 756:         BigDecimal oldLock = p.profitLockPrice();
 757:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(walletSettings().getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 758:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = p.highest() == null ? p.entryPrice() : p.highest();
 759:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 760:         ShadowPosition withExtensionHigh = p.withLock(extensionHighest, p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(),
 761:                 p.profitLockState(), p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 762:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 763:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 764:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 765:                 ShadowPosition rebasing = withExtensionHigh.withLock(extensionHighest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 766:                         at == null ? Instant.now() : at);
 767:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 768:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 769:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), rebasing.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 770:                         extensionHighest, progress, oldLock, rebasing.profitLockPrice(), activation);
 771:                 return rebasing;
 772:             }
 773:             // FIX-120 Production/Replay parity: the old executable lock belongs to the
 774:             // superseded TP geometry. If the extended winner still qualifies, establish a
 775:             // fresh geometry baseline. Raw extensionPrice is current; extensionHighest is the
 776:             // historical-best seed; previousActive/previousLock are deliberately discarded.
 777:             WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 778:             BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
 779:             ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 780:                     p.entryPrice(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
 781:                     false, null, settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 782:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 783:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 784:             if (rebased.active()) {
 785:                 ShadowPosition active = withExtensionHigh.withLock(rebased.highestPrice(), true, rebased.lockPrice(), ProfitLockState.ACTIVE, null);
 786:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 787:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 788:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), active.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 789:                         rebased.highestPrice(), rebased.progressPercent(), oldLock, rebased.lockPrice(), activation);
 790:                 return active;
 791:             }
 792:             ShadowPosition rebasing = withExtensionHigh.withLock(rebased.highestPrice(), true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 793:                     at == null ? Instant.now() : at);
 794:             log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 795:                             "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 796:                     p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), rebasing.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 797:                     rebased.highestPrice(), rebased.progressPercent(), oldLock, rebasing.profitLockPrice(), activation);
 798:             return rebasing;
 799:         }
 800:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 801:             return withExtensionHigh.withLock(extensionHighest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 802:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 803:         }
 804:         return withExtensionHigh;
 805:     }
 210:      * FIX-120 correction/refinement to FIX-118. Called in the same transaction that persists the new TP.
 211:      * A TP change invalidates the prior executable lock as a calculation baseline. If current
 212:      * best progress remains at/above activation, stay ACTIVE but derive a fresh lock from the
 213:      * new geometry using raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as the historical
 214:      * best seed. If progress falls below activation, preserve the historical lock for audit but
 215:      * make it non-executable in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE until the current geometry requalifies.
 216:      */
 217:     public ExtensionTransition onTakeProfitExtended(WalletManagedPosition position, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant changedAt) {
 218:         if (position == null || newTarget == null || position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt() == null) {
 219:             return ExtensionTransition.none();
 220:         }
 221:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
 222:         BigDecimal oldTarget = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 223:         BigDecimal oldLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
 224:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null
 225:                 ? position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt()
 226:                 : position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
 227:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) {
 228:             extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 229:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(extensionHighest);
 230:         }
 231:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 232:         BigDecimal activation = configuredProfile(settings()).activationPercent();
 233:         ProfitLockState nextState = previousState;
 234:         // TP changed now, so persist progress against the new geometry immediately even though
 235:         // the extension tick returns before the next normal Profit Lock evaluation.
 236:         position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
 237: 
 238:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 239:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 240:                 nextState = ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 241:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 242:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt);
 243:                 // Legacy compatibility flag deliberately stays true; ProfitLockState is the sole
 244:                 // authority for whether the persisted historical lock may execute.
 245:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 246:             } else {
 247:                 // FIX-120: the extension still leaves the winner beyond activation, but the old
 248:                 // lock belongs to the superseded TP geometry. Establish a fresh geometry baseline.
 249:                 // Deliberately pass raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as previous
 250:                 // highest: ProfitLockPolicy then sees the real boundary tick plus the authoritative
 251:                 // best price, while previousActive/previousLock are intentionally discarded.
 252:                 ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings());
 253:                 BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
 254:                 ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 255:                         position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
 256:                         false, null, true, profile.activationPercent(), profile.initialLockPercent(), profile.trailStepPercent());
 257:                 nextState = rebased.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 258:                 position.setHighestPriceUsdt(rebased.highestPrice());
 259:                 position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(rebased.progressPercent());
 260:                 position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(rebased.lockPrice());
 261:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 262:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 263:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(nextState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 264:                         ? (changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt)
 265:                         : null);
 266:                 progress = rebased.progressPercent();
 267:             }
 268:         } else if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 269:             // Repeated TP extensions keep the rebase active and refresh progress against the
 270:             // newest geometry without resetting the original rebase start timestamp.
 271:         }
 272: 
 273:         log.info("[FIX-120][PRODUCTION][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, symbol={}, previousState={}, newState={}, " +
 274:                         "oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, " +
 275:                         "activationPct={}, geometryRebased={}",
 276:                 position.getId(), position.getSymbol(), previousState, nextState, oldTarget, newTarget,
 277:                 extensionPrice, position.getHighestPriceUsdt(), progress,
 278:                 oldLock, position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(),
 279:                 activation, previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE);
 280: 
 281:         return new ExtensionTransition(previousState, nextState, progress, activation);
 282:     }

 284:     private ProfitLockState normalizedState(WalletManagedPosition position) {
   1: package com.crypto.regression.service;

  25: /**
  26:  * FIX-11Q ƒ?" Replay-only one-candle continuation grace experiment.
  27:  *
  28:  * The Production-parity Shadow position always remains authoritative. This observer starts a
  29:  * separate counterfactual position only after the existing PositionContinuationPolicy has already
  30:  * returned FAIL at a reached take-profit checkpoint. Existing continuation PASS paths therefore
  31:  * remain untouched and continue to extend take profit exactly as before.
  32:  *
  33:  * The counterfactual holds through one specific NEUTRAL 1m candle without moving TP. The grace is
  34:  * tied to that candle (not one live-price tick), may be consumed only once per position lifetime,
  35:  * and normal shared continuation/exit policies resume after a new 1m candle arrives.
  36:  */
  37: @Slf4j
  38: @Service
  39: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  40: public class OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver {
  41:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  42: 
  43:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  44:     private final PositionContinuationPolicy continuationPolicy;
  45:     private final PositionExitPolicy exitPolicy;
  46:     private final ProfitLockPolicy profitLockPolicy;
  47:     private final TradeExecutionValidationService executionValidationService;
  48:     private final WalletSettingsRepository walletSettingsRepository;
  49: 
  50:     private final Map<Long, Variant> activeByRun = new HashMap<>();
  51:     private final Map<Long, java.util.Set<Long>> consumedByRun = new HashMap<>();
  52: 
  53:     public synchronized void startRun(long runId, String symbol) {
  54:         activeByRun.remove(runId);
  55:         consumedByRun.put(runId, new java.util.HashSet<>());
  56:         log.info("FIX11Q_GRACE_START runId={} symbol={} mode=REPLAY_COUNTERFACTUAL productionMutation=false baselineReplayMutation=false", runId, symbol);
  57:     }
  58: 
  59:     public synchronized void observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
  60:             long runId, String symbol, long positionId, Instant observedAt, BigDecimal price,
  61:             Instant entryTime, BigDecimal entryPrice, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal cost,
  62:             BigDecimal stopLoss, BigDecimal takeProfit, BigDecimal highest,
  63:             boolean profitLockActive, BigDecimal profitLockPrice,
  64:             int entryScore, int entryConfidence, int entryTrend, int entryStructure,
  65:             int entryMomentum, int entryVolume,
  66:             TradeSignal current1m, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  67:             String baselineExplanation) {
  68: 
  69:         if (activeByRun.containsKey(runId) || consumedByRun.getOrDefault(runId, java.util.Set.of()).contains(positionId)) return;
  70:         if (!eligible(current1m, fiveMinute, oneHour, entryMomentum)) return;
  71: 
  72:         Instant candleIdentity = candleIdentity(current1m);
  73:         if (candleIdentity == null) return;
  74:         BigDecimal baselinePnl = price.multiply(quantity, MC).subtract(cost, MC);
  75:         BigDecimal baselinePnlPct = percentage(entryPrice, price);
  76:         BigDecimal safeHighest = highest == null ? price : highest.max(price);
  77: 
  78:         Variant v = new Variant(positionId, symbol, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, stopLoss,
  79:                 takeProfit, safeHighest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice,
  80:                 entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
  81:                 candleIdentity, current1m == null ? null : current1m.getId(), observedAt, price,
  82:                 baselinePnl, baselinePnlPct, price, price, current1m, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  83:         activeByRun.put(runId, v);
  84:         consumedByRun.computeIfAbsent(runId, k -> new java.util.HashSet<>()).add(positionId);
  85: 
  86:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
  87:                 INSERT INTO one_candle_continuation_grace_test
  88:                 (test_run_id,position_test_id,symbol,grace_at,grace_1m_signal_id,grace_candle_time,
  89:                  price_at_grace,entry_price,take_profit_before_grace,take_profit_after_grace,
  90:                  current_1m_decision,five_minute_decision,one_hour_decision,trend_score,trend_floor,
  91:                  momentum_score,momentum_floor,baseline_action,baseline_exit_price,baseline_pnl_usdt,
  92:                  baseline_pnl_percent,status,baseline_explanation)
  93:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
  94:                 """, runId, positionId, symbol, Timestamp.from(observedAt), v.graceSignalId(), Timestamp.from(candleIdentity),
  95:                 price, entryPrice, takeProfit, takeProfit, name(current1m.getDecision()), name(fiveMinute.getDecision()),
  96:                 name(oneHour.getDecision()), current1m.getTrendScore(), trendFloor(entryTrend),
  97:                 current1m.getMomentumScore(), momentumFloor(entryMomentum), "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", price,
  98:                 baselinePnl, baselinePnlPct, "ACTIVE", baselineExplanation);
  99: 
 100:         log.info("FIX11Q_GRACE_ACTIVATED runId={} positionId={} symbol={} observedAt={} candle={} price={} tp={} current={} five={} one={} trend={}/{} momentum={}/{} action=HOLD_TARGET_UNCHANGED",
 101:                 runId, positionId, symbol, observedAt, candleIdentity, price, takeProfit,
 102:                 name(current1m.getDecision()), name(fiveMinute.getDecision()), name(oneHour.getDecision()),
 103:                 current1m.getTrendScore(), trendFloor(entryTrend), current1m.getMomentumScore(), momentumFloor(entryMomentum));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public synchronized void onSignal(long runId, TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal latest1m, TradeSignal latest5m, TradeSignal latest1h) {
 107:         Variant v = activeByRun.get(runId);
 108:         if (v == null || signal == null) return;
 109:         if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next1mDecision() == null && !sameGraceCandle(v, signal)) {
 110:             v = v.withNext1m(name(signal.getDecision()));
 111:         }
 112:         if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next5mDecision() == null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null && signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) {
 113:             v = v.withNext5m(name(signal.getDecision()));
 114:         }
 115:         if ("1h".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next1hDecision() == null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null && signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) {
 116:             v = v.withNext1h(name(signal.getDecision()));
 117:         }
 118:         activeByRun.put(runId, v.withContext(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h));
 119:     }
 120: 
 121:     public synchronized void onPrice(long runId, String symbol, MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent event,
 122:                                      TradeSignal latest1m, TradeSignal latest5m, TradeSignal latest1h) {
 123:         Variant v = activeByRun.get(runId);
 124:         if (v == null || event == null || event.price() == null || !v.symbol().equals(symbol)) return;
 125:         if (!event.observedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) return;
 126: 
 127:         BigDecimal price = event.price();
 128:         v = v.withContext(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h).withPath(price);
 129:         boolean sameGraceCandle = sameGraceCandle(v, latest1m);
 130: 
 131:         // Hard protection remains active even during grace; FIX-11Q suppresses only the baseline
 132:         // TAKE_PROFIT exit caused by transient continuation failure.
 133:         if (v.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(v.stopLoss()) <= 0) {
 134:             close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "STOP_LOSS", "Stop loss reached during/after grace."); return;
 135:         }
 136: 
 137:         v = updateProfitLock(v, price);
 138:         if (v.profitLockActive() && v.profitLockPrice() != null && price.compareTo(v.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
 139:             BigDecimal hardFloor = v.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
 140:             if (price.compareTo(hardFloor) < 0) {
 141:                 close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", "Profit-lock hard floor breached in FIX-11Q counterfactual."); return;
 142:             }
 143:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lock = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 144:             if (lock.exit()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, lock.code(), lock.explanation()); return; }
 145:         }
 146: 
 147:         if (v.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(v.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 148:             if (sameGraceCandle) {
 149:                 activeByRun.put(runId, v); // hold entire candle; target intentionally unchanged
 150:                 return;
 151:             }
 152:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 153:                     latest1m, latest5m, latest1h, v.entryTrend(), v.entryStructure(), v.entryMomentum(), v.entryVolume(),
 154:                     v.entryConfidence(), v.entryScore());
 155:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 156:                 BigDecimal distance = v.takeProfit().subtract(v.entryPrice());
 157:                 BigDecimal newTarget = v.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 158:                 activeByRun.put(runId, v.withTakeProfit(newTarget));
 159:                 log.info("FIX11Q_VARIANT_TP_EXTENDED runId={} positionId={} oldTp={} newTp={} reason={}", runId, v.positionId(), v.takeProfit(), newTarget, continuation.explanation());
 160:                 return;
 161:             }
 162:             close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation()); return;
 163:         }
 164: 
 165:         if (!sameGraceCandle) {
 166:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normal = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 167:             if (normal.exit()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, normal.code(), normal.explanation()); return; }
 168:             if (latest1m != null && "1m".equals(latest1m.getInterval()) && bearish(latest1m.getDecision())) {
 169:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validated = executionValidationService.validateSell(latest1m);
 170:                 if (validated.allowed()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, validated.code(), validated.explanation()); return; }
 171:             }
 172:         }
 173:         activeByRun.put(runId, v);
 174:     }
 175: 
 176:     public synchronized void finishRun(long runId, String symbol, Instant endTime) {
 177:         Variant v = activeByRun.remove(runId);
 178:         if (v != null) {
 179:             BigDecimal pnl = v.lastPrice().multiply(v.quantity(), MC).subtract(v.cost(), MC);
 180:             updateRow(runId, v, endTime, v.lastPrice(), "OPEN_AT_WINDOW_END", pnl, percentage(v.entryPrice(), v.lastPrice()));
 181:         }
 182:         Integer count = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM one_candle_continuation_grace_test WHERE test_run_id=?", Integer.class, runId);
 183:         log.info("FIX11Q_GRACE_SUMMARY runId={} symbol={} candidates={} mode=REPLAY_COUNTERFACTUAL", runId, symbol, count == null ? 0 : count);
 184:         consumedByRun.remove(runId);
 185:     }
 186: 
 187:     private boolean eligible(TradeSignal one, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal hour, int entryMomentum) {
 188:         return one != null && five != null && hour != null
 189:                 && one.getDecision() == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 190:                 && bullish(hour.getDecision()) && !bearish(five.getDecision())
 191:                 && one.getMomentumScore() >= momentumFloor(entryMomentum);
 192:     }
 193: 
 194:     private Variant updateProfitLock(Variant v, BigDecimal price) {
 195:         WalletSettings s = walletSettingsRepository.findById(1L).orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 196:                 .id(1L).dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true).profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 197:                 .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40)).profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10)).build());
 198:         ProfitLockPolicy.State st = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(v.entryPrice(), v.takeProfit(), price, v.highest(),
 199:                 v.profitLockActive(), v.profitLockPrice(), s.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(),
 200:                 nvl(s.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70)),
 201:                 nvl(s.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 202:                 nvl(s.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 203:         return v.withLock(st.highestPrice(), st.active(), st.lockPrice());
 204:     }

 206:     private void close(long runId, Variant v, Instant at, BigDecimal price, String reason, String explanation) {
 207:         BigDecimal pnl = price.multiply(v.quantity(), MC).subtract(v.cost(), MC);
 208:         updateRow(runId, v, at, price, reason, pnl, percentage(v.entryPrice(), price));
 209:         activeByRun.remove(runId);
 210:         log.info("FIX11Q_VARIANT_CLOSED runId={} positionId={} symbol={} at={} price={} reason={} baselinePnl={} variantPnl={} delta={}",
 211:                 runId, v.positionId(), v.symbol(), at, price, reason, v.baselinePnl(), pnl, pnl.subtract(v.baselinePnl(), MC));
 212:     }

 215:         String result = result(v);

 178:     /**
 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
 181:      */
 182:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
 184:         if (snapshot == null) {
 185:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
 186:         }
 187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 189:         try {
 190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
 191:         } finally {
 192:             if (profiler != null) {
 193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196:     }

 199:      * FIX-11M: Called only by RegressionTestWorker around the fresh-signal stage.
 200:      * This activates timing observation; it does not alter buildSignal inputs, outputs,
 201:      * ordering, thresholds, persistence, or any Production execution path.
 202:      */
 203:     public void beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
 204:         replayAnalysisProfiler.set(new ReplayAnalysisProfiler(runId));
 205:     }

 208:      * FIX-11M: Emit one aggregate Replay-only summary and always clear thread-local state.

 210:     public void finishReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
 211:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 212:         replayAnalysisProfiler.remove();
 213:         if (profiler == null) {
 214:             log.warn("FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE_MISSING run={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false", runId);
 215:             return;

 220:     private TradeSignal buildSignal(IndicatorSnapshot i, Instant signalGeneratedAt, boolean historicalReplay,
 221:                                     boolean regressionReplay) {
 222:         String symbol = i.symbol();
 223:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = regressionReplay ? replayAnalysisProfiler.get() : null;
 224:         long profileStartedNs;
 225: 
 226:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 227:         boolean sentimentEnabled = sentimentService.isEnabled();
 228:         SentimentOverview sentimentOverview = sentimentService.overviewAsOf(symbol, signalGeneratedAt);
 229:         BigDecimal sentiment = sentimentOverview.weightedScore();
 230:         boolean sentimentAvailable = hasUsableSentimentCoverage(sentimentEnabled, sentimentOverview);
 231:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.SENTIMENT, profileStartedNs);
 232: 
 233:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 234:         MarketFundamental fundamental = fundamentalService.latestAsOf(symbol, signalGeneratedAt).orElse(null);
 235:         boolean fundamentalAvailable = fundamentalService.isAvailable(fundamental, signalGeneratedAt);
 236:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.FUNDAMENTALS, profileStartedNs);
 237: 
 238:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 239:         IndicatorSnapshot previous = previousSnapshot(i);
 240:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.PREVIOUS_SNAPSHOT, profileStartedNs);
 241: 
 242:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 243:         TrendStructureResult trendStructure = trendStructureService.evaluate(i);
 244:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.TREND_STRUCTURE, profileStartedNs);
 245: 
 246:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 247:         MovingAverageBreakdown movingAverages = movingAverageScore(i, previous, trendStructure);
 248:         MomentumBreakdown momentumBreakdown = momentumScore(i);
 249:         BandsVolumeBreakdown bandsVolume = bandsVolumeScore(i, previous);
 250:         int trend = movingAverages.total();
 251:         int volume = bandsVolume.total();
 252:         int momentum = momentumBreakdown.total();
 253:         int sentimentPoints = sentimentAvailable ? sentimentScore(sentiment) : 0;
 254:         FundamentalScoreResult fundamentalBreakdown = fundamentalScoringService.score(fundamentalAvailable ? fundamental : null);
 255:         int fundamentals = fundamentalAvailable ? fundamentalBreakdown.total() : 0;
 256:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.BASE_SCORING, profileStartedNs);
 257: 
 258:         int baseRawTotal = trend + volume + momentum + sentimentPoints + fundamentals;
 259:         int baseMaximumAvailableScore = MAX_TREND_SCORE
 260:                 + MAX_VOLUME_SCORE
 261:                 + MAX_MOMENTUM_SCORE
 262:                 + (fundamentalAvailable ? MAX_FUNDAMENTAL_SCORE : 0)
 263:                 + (sentimentAvailable ? MAX_SENTIMENT_SCORE : 0);
 264: 
 265:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 266:         AtrRiskAssessment atrRisk = atrRiskService.assess(i);
 267:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.ATR_RISK, profileStartedNs);
 268: 
 269:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 270:         MarketRegimeAssessment detectedRegimeAssessment = marketRegimeService.assess(i, trendStructure);
 271:         RegimeStateService.Decision regimeState = regimeStateService.apply(
 272:                 symbol, i.intervalCode(), i.candleOpenTime(), detectedRegimeAssessment);
 273:         MarketRegimeAssessment regimeAssessment = regimeState.effectiveAssessment();
 274:         MarketRegime marketRegime = regimeAssessment.regime();
 275:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.REGIME, profileStartedNs);
 276: 
 277:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 278:         MarketContextSnapshot marketContext = marketContextService.build(
 279:                 i, atrRisk, sentimentOverview, sentimentEnabled, signalGeneratedAt, historicalReplay);
 280:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.MARKET_CONTEXT, profileStartedNs);
 281: 
 282:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 283:         StrategyProfile strategyProfile = marketStrategyService.select(regimeAssessment, marketContext);
 284:         atrRisk = atrRiskService.applyStrategyEntryPlan(atrRisk, i, strategyProfile.strategy());
 285:         StrategyScoreResult strategyScore = marketStrategyService.score(
 286:                 strategyProfile,
 287:                 trend,
 288:                 volume,
 289:                 momentum,
 290:                 sentimentPoints,
 291:                 fundamentals,
 292:                 sentimentAvailable,
 293:                 fundamentalAvailable
 294:         );
 295:         strategyScore = marketStrategyService.constrainBreakoutCandidate(regimeAssessment, strategyScore);
 296:         strategyScore = marketStrategyService.promoteEarlyBreakout(
 297:                 strategyProfile, strategyScore, marketContext, atrRisk, regimeAssessment, trendStructure);
 298:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.STRATEGY, profileStartedNs);
 299: 
 300:         int rawTotal = strategyScore.rawScore();
 301:         int maximumAvailableScore = strategyScore.maximumScore();
 302:         int total = strategyScore.normalizedScore();
 303:         SignalDecision baseDecision = strategyScore.decision();
 304:         SignalDecision atrAdjustedDecision = baseDecision;
 305:         // ATR controls entry execution, not bullish direction. Keep BUY as BUY for
 306:         // REDUCED_POSITION, PULLBACK_ENTRY and WAIT_FOR_RETRACEMENT. Only an
 307:         // extreme NO_ENTRY extension downgrades the directional recommendation.
 308:         if (atrRisk.entryType() == com.crypto.domain.AtrEntryType.NO_ENTRY
 309:                 && (atrAdjustedDecision == SignalDecision.BUY || atrAdjustedDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY)) {
 310:             atrAdjustedDecision = SignalDecision.WATCH;
 311:         }
 312: 
 313:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 314:         MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult confluence = multiTimeframeConfluenceService.evaluate(
 315:                 symbol, i.intervalCode(), atrAdjustedDecision, signalGeneratedAt, strategyProfile.strategy()
 316:         );
 317:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.MULTI_TIMEFRAME, profileStartedNs);
 318: 
 319:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 320:         BtcMarketContextResult btcContext = btcMarketContextService.evaluate(
 321:                 symbol, i.intervalCode(), confluence.finalDecision(), confluence.entryAllowed(), signalGeneratedAt
 322:         );
 323:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.BTC_CONTEXT, profileStartedNs);
 324: 
 325:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 326:         // FIX-11N: Regression Replay must never issue live Binance futures requests for a
 327:         // historical candle. Keep normal Production and FIX-043 Production recovery exactly
 328:         // on the pre-existing live evaluate(...) path; only analyzeForRegression() selects
 329:         // evaluateHistorical(...). This removes Replay-only network I/O without changing any
 330:         // Production derivatives behavior, thresholds, ordering, wallet logic or execution.
 506:      * FIX-11S: one read-only chart payload for an entire Replay run. Replay executions are
 507:      * taken from the isolated wallet_execution_test rows; Production executions are only
 508:      * queried for the same symbol and requested Replay window. An empty Production result
 509:      * is a normal case and is deliberately returned as [] rather than null.
 510:      */
 511:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 512:     public Map<String, Object> allTradesChart(long runId, String interval) {
 513:         List<Map<String, Object>> runs = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 514:                 SELECT id, symbol, start_time, end_time
 515:                 FROM analysis_test_run
 516:                 WHERE id = ?
 517:                 """, runId);
 518:         if (runs.isEmpty()) {
 519:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Replay run not found.");
 520:         }
 521: 
 522:         Map<String, Object> run = runs.get(0);
 523:         String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol")).trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 524:         Timestamp start = (Timestamp) run.get("start_time");
 525:         Timestamp end = (Timestamp) run.get("end_time");
 526:         String normalizedInterval = switch (interval == null ? "5m" : interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
 527:             case "1m", "5m", "1h", "4h" -> interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
 528:             default -> "5m";
 529:         };
 530: 
 531:         List<Map<String, Object>> candles = candleReader(jdbcTemplate).queryForList("""
 532:                 SELECT open_time, open_price, high_price, low_price, close_price, volume
 533:                 FROM candle
 534:                 WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?
 535:                 ORDER BY open_time ASC
 536:                 """, symbol, normalizedInterval, start, end);
 537: 
 538:         List<Map<String, Object>> replayExecutions = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 539:                 SELECT id, side, execution_time, execution_price, quantity, notional_usdt,
 540:                        position_percent, signal_interval, signal_decision, execution_source,
 541:                        execution_code, execution_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent
 542:                 FROM wallet_execution_test
 543:                 WHERE test_run_id=? AND symbol=?
 544:                   AND execution_time BETWEEN ? AND ?
 545:                   AND UPPER(side) IN ('BUY','SELL')
 546:                 ORDER BY execution_time ASC, id ASC
 547:                 """, runId, symbol, start, end);
 548: 
 549:         List<Map<String, Object>> productionExecutions = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 550:                 SELECT id, signal_id, side, executed_at AS execution_time, price_usdt AS execution_price,
 551:                        quantity, gross_amount_usdt, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent,
 552:                        execution_type, execution_reason, execution_message
 553:                 FROM wallet_trade
 554:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED'
 555:                   AND executed_at BETWEEN ? AND ?
 556:                   AND UPPER(side) IN ('BUY','SELL')
 557:                 ORDER BY executed_at ASC, id ASC
 558:                 """, symbol, start, end);
 559: 
 560:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 561:         result.put("runId", runId);
 562:         result.put("symbol", symbol);
 563:         result.put("interval", normalizedInterval);
 564:         result.put("from", start);
 565:         result.put("to", end);
 566:         result.put("candles", candles);
 567:         result.put("replayExecutions", replayExecutions);
 568:         result.put("productionExecutions", productionExecutions);
 569:         return result;
 570:     }

 572:     /** FIX-11K Phase A: read-only replay observations; never Production execution. */
 573:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 574:     public List<Map<String, Object>> defensiveRiskReductionObservations(long runId) {
 575:         return jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 576:                 SELECT id, position_test_id, symbol, observed_at, source_signal_id,
 577:                        current_price, entry_price, highest_price_since_entry,
 116:                 // lock remains stored but is non-executable until new-geometry activation is
 117:                 // earned again.
 118:                 DynamicProfitLockService.ExtensionTransition fix118Transition =
 119:                         dynamicProfitLockService.onTakeProfitExtended(managed, newTarget, price, changedAt);
 120:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][TP_EXTENSION] positionId={}, symbol={}, marketPrice={}, entry={}, oldTp={}, newTp={}, " +
 121:                                 "highest={}, profitLockActive={}, profitLockPrice={}, previousState={}, state={}, " +
 122:                                 "newGeometryProgressPct={}, activationPct={}",
 123:                         managed.getId(), symbol, price, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), oldTarget, newTarget,
 124:                         managed.getHighestPriceUsdt(), managed.isProfitLockActive(), managed.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(),
 125:                         fix118Transition.previousState(), fix118Transition.state(), fix118Transition.progressPercent(),
 126:                         fix118Transition.activationPercent());
 127: 
 128:                 managed.setTakeProfitUsdt(newTarget);
 129:                 // FIX-11T: TP extension changes the planned entry->TP distance. Any old Near-TP
 130:                 // arm/rejection was relative to the superseded target and must be restarted.
 131:                 resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(managed);
 132:                 managed.setUpdatedAt(changedAt);
 133:                 managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 134: 
 135:                 // FIX-067: Production has two persisted views of the same open lifecycle:
 136:                 // wallet_managed_position drives live protection, while paper_position is also
 137:                 // evaluated when a fresh TradeSignal arrives.  Before this fix only the managed
 138:                 // position received a TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED update.  The next 1m signal could
 139:                 // therefore see the stale ORIGINAL paper TP and close the wallet early.
 140:                 // PEPE wallet #833 / managed position #552 proved the race: managed TP was
 141:                 // extended 0.000004146897 -> 0.000004165346, but the signal path sold at
 142:                 // 0.000004150 because paper_position still held 0.000004146897.
 143:                 // Keep both Production state holders synchronized inside this same transaction.
 144:                 PaperPosition synchronizedPaper = paperPositionRepository
 145:                         .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN)
 146:                         .orElse(null);
 147:                 if (synchronizedPaper != null) {
 148:                     synchronizedPaper.setTakeProfit(newTarget);
 149:                     paperPositionRepository.save(synchronizedPaper);
 150:                 }
 151: 
 152:                 // FIX-053: Persist every TP revision so Dashboard can show the actual
 153:                 // Production management path instead of only the latest target value.
 154:                 positionManagementEventRepository.save(PositionManagementEvent.builder()
 155:                         .walletPositionId(managed.getId())
 156:                         .symbol(symbol)
 157:                         .eventType("TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED")
 158:                         .oldValueUsdt(oldTarget)
 159:                         .newValueUsdt(newTarget)
 160:                         .marketPriceUsdt(price)
 161:                         .reason(continuation.explanation())
 162:                         .occurredAt(changedAt)
 163:                         .build());
 164:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT extended: symbol={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, reason={}", symbol, oldTarget, newTarget, continuation.explanation());
 165:             } else if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 166:                     symbol, price, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation())) {
 167:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), one);
 168:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT executed: symbol={}, price={}, target={}", symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 169:             }
 170:             return;
 171:         }
 172: 
 173:         // Stop loss is absolute and is never softened by continuation logic.
 174:         if (managed.getStopLossUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
 175:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 176:                     symbol, price, "STOP_LOSS",
 177:                     "Live price " + price + " reached stop loss " + managed.getStopLossUsdt())) {
 178:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.STOPPED, "STOP_LOSS",
 179:                         "Live market price reached the configured stop loss.", one);
 180:                 log.info("Live STOP_LOSS executed: symbol={}, price={}, stop={}", symbol, price, managed.getStopLossUsdt());
 181:             }
 182:             return;
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation lock = dynamicProfitLockService.evaluatePrice(symbol, price);
 186: 
 187:         // FIX-118: during TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the superseded lock is intentionally
 188:         // non-executable, but earned profit is not left completely unprotected. Stop Loss
 189:         // has already had absolute priority above; this standalone floor is the next safety
 190:         // authority and does not depend on the old lock being triggered.
 191:         if (lock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 192:             String reason = lock.explanation();
 193:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}, target={}",
 194:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.hardProfitFloor(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 195:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 196:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 197:             }
 198:             return;
 199:         }
 200: 
 201:         if (lock.active() || lock.triggered() || lock.rebasing()) {
 202:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_PATH] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, target={}, highest={}, active={}, " +
 203:                             "progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 204:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(), lock.highestPrice(), lock.active(),
 205:                     lock.progressPercent(), lock.activationPercent(), lock.lockPrice(), lock.state(), lock.triggered());
 206:         }
 207:         if (lock.triggered()) {
 208:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_TRIGGER] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, target={}",
 209:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lock.progressPercent(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 210:             BigDecimal hardProfitFloor = managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
 211:             if (price.compareTo(hardProfitFloor) < 0) {
 212:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " Hard protected-profit floor " + hardProfitFloor + " was breached.";
 213:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 214:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 215:                 }
 216:                 return;
 217:             }
 218: 
 219:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(one, five, hour);
 220:             if (lockDecision.exit()) {
 221:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " " + lockDecision.explanation();
 222:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, lockDecision.code(), reason)) {
 223:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, lockDecision.code(), reason, one);
 224:                     log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK exit: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 225:                             symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.code());
 226:                 }
 227:                 return;
 228:             }
 229:             log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK protected hold: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 230:                     symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.explanation());
 231:         }
 232: 
 233:         // Normal signal exits remain active even when Profit Lock is disabled. Higher-timeframe
 234:         // failure can therefore close a winner instead of waiting forever for an exact 1m SELL event.
 235:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(one, five, hour);
 236:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 237:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation())) {
 238:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation(), one);
 239:                 log.info("Live signal-driven exit: symbol={}, price={}, reason={}", symbol, price, normalExit.code());
 240:             }
 241:             // FIX-11T: any higher-priority position action owns this evaluation cycle.
 242:             // Near-TP must never race another SELL or quantity-changing decision on the same tick.
 243:             return;
 244:         }
 245: 
 246:         // FIX-11T: Near-TP Failure Protection is deliberately LAST. TP/SL/profit-lock and
 247:         // existing signal exits above retain their exact authority and always win the cycle.
 248:         evaluateNearTpProtection(managed, price, one, five);
 249:     }
 250: 
 251:     private void evaluateNearTpProtection(WalletManagedPosition managed, BigDecimal price,
 252:                                           TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 253:         // FIX-11T evidence is explicitly time-safe. Normal Production logic above keeps its
 254:         // existing signal lookup unchanged; Near-TP alone falls back to an at-or-before query
 255:         // if the repository's newest row is future-dated relative to this live evaluation.

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Preparation is not complete or implementation-ready by its own status: P01–P07 remain pending; E03–E10 are uncompleted; no approved canonical FIX-156 specification exists. The required canonical file is still RPR-0.4-DRAFT, not RPR-0.9.11.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The stated decision-order test oracle is missing. T156-02a/b and logging require “Section 3.3.1,” but this 400-line preparation contains no such section. The test suite therefore has no frozen authoritative precedence order to implement or validate.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The new `fix122_evaluation` exception is materially inaccurate. The table-specific delete is scoped to non-null `test_run_id`, but the actual “Delete Data” operation purges every Replay run and resets `analysis_test_run` AUTO_INCREMENT. Run IDs can therefore be reused; the code explicitly warns of this. A rule allowing only rows of the “active Replay run” does not describe the existing purge, and `runId` is not a durable unique correlation identity.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The proposed dated fixture contract is incomplete against the actual analysis path. `analyzeForRegression` uses sentiment, fundamentals, prior indicators, ATR risk, regime state, market context, dynamic strategy properties, confluence, BTC context, order-book liquidity, derivatives, and final-decision services. R156-04 explicitly specifies only a BUY/dynamic-profit-lock profile and D28 exchange constraint; D53/D54 cover only sentiment configuration and prior snapshots. No complete as-of configuration/input inventory or per-input refusal rule is stated.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Major current Replay boundary violations remain real: direct `wallet_settings` reads, shared BUY validation, live Binance `exchangeInfo`, shared diagnostics writes, `wallet_trade` chart reads, signal-price fallback, and experimental observers. These are valid target gaps, not evidence that an implementation is ready.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The current price projection orders SQL by `(observed_at, id)` but discards both ID and delivery status from `PriceEvent`; `ShadowProductionReplayService` then re-sorts only by time. R156-06 correctly identifies the required repair, but current code cannot meet equal-time ordering or evidence provenance requirements.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Claude’s older assertion that this preparation lacks a logging registry is no longer correct: the current document has one at §6. That correction does not cure the missing Section 3.3.1 oracle or pending gates.

## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Do not freeze R156-05’s shared-table exception without a stable immutable run UUID and a concurrency/retention policy. Otherwise purge/reuse can make certificates and logs refer to the wrong historical run.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Do not implement mirrors until the document names every analysis-time configuration and external/historical input, its provenance, as-of availability rule, fingerprint, and refusal/non-comparable outcome. Otherwise current values can silently produce look-ahead bias.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Static reachability tests are necessary but insufficient alone; the frozen contract must define the exact allowed graph and verify asynchronous, reflection/dynamic-SQL, and controller/output paths.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The proposed 99% claim remains statistically underdefined until the confidence method, denominator, exclusion cap, and whether thresholds apply overall or per exit class are frozen before acceptance data is opened.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No database, production system, runtime service, or private logs were accessed.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Historical sources for dated `wallet_settings`, dynamic strategy/scoring properties, and Binance exchange constraints remain unproven. The plan must refuse or classify periods where they cannot be reconstructed.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The external RPR-0.9.11 draft was not established as the canonical workspace contract; its claimed hash cannot substitute for P01.

## FINDINGS FOR JEAN

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** This is a useful preparation inventory, but it is not freeze- or implementation-ready.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Before any authorization, complete P01–P07/E03–E10; put the exact reviewed specification at the mandated canonical path; add the missing precedence oracle; correct §4.2a for global purge/run-ID reuse; and fully register as-of analysis inputs and refusals.
- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Require the acceptance protocol to prevent selective exclusions, look-ahead contamination, and Replay/Production drift before interpreting any parity percentage.
